Project Avalanche — third-party notices of the dedicated server

avalanche-server (Project Avalanche's dedicated multiplayer server) contains the third-party software listed below, for
Windows x64 and Linux x64. Each entry names the package, its version, its license expression and the license it is
used under (one side of an "OR"), its authors and repository, and reproduces the license texts the package ships;
where a package ships no text for its license, the standard text follows with the package's authors as the copyright
holders. Texts that occur more than once are printed once, at the end, and referred to by their number. The patched
packages (lightyear's crates, bevy_replicon) are the published releases with the changes their source files mark
"Project Avalanche patch".

SSX is (c) Electronic Arts. Project Avalanche is an unofficial port; the server contains none of the game's data.

Generated from Cargo.lock by Engine/tools/notices/notices.py; do not edit by hand.

342 packages.

--------------

    means Covered Software of a particular Contributor.

    the notice in Exhibit A, the Executable Form of such Source Code

        in Exhibit B to the Covered Software; or

    means any form of the work other than Source Code Form.

    means a work that combines Covered Software with other material, in
    a separate file or files, that is not Covered Software.

1.8. "License"
    means this document.

1.9. "Licensable"
    means having the right to grant, to the maximum extent possible,
    whether at the time of the initial grant or subsequently, any and

    means any of the following:

    (a) any file in Source Code Form that results from an addition to,

        Software; or

    (b) any new file in Source Code Form that contains any Covered

    means any patent claim(s), including without limitation, method,
